Implement the following plan:

# Sandbox CPU & Memory Sparkline Graphs

## Context
Stats are currently fetched on-demand from Docker and shown as text on the dashboard. No historical data is persisted. We want small inline sparkline graphs (CPU and MEM, last 30 min) on the sandbox show page for running sandboxes, combining persisted history (survives page reload) with live real-time polling.

## Architecture
- **Background job** collects CPU/MEM every 5 min → DB (historical backbone)
- **Show page** polls the existing `stats` endpoint every 5s → appends live points in JS
- **On page load**: fetch persisted history from DB, then start live polling — seamless merge
- **SVG sparklines** rendered client-side by a Stimulus controller (no chart library)

## Implementation Steps

### 1. Migration: `container_metrics` table
```ruby
create_table :container_metrics do |t|
  t.references :sandbox, null: false, foreign_key: true
  t.float :cpu_percent, null: false
  t.float :memory_mb, null: false
  t.datetime :recorded_at, null: false
end
add_index :container_metrics, [:sandbox_id, :recorded_at]
```
**File:** `db/migrate/TIMESTAMP_create_container_metrics.rb`

### 2. Model: `ContainerMetric`
- `belongs_to :sandbox`
- Scope `recent`: `where("recorded_at > ?", 30.minutes.ago).order(:recorded_at)`
- No timestamps needed (we have `recorded_at`)

**File:** `app/models/container_metric.rb`
**Edit:** `app/models/sandbox.rb` — add `has_many :container_metrics, dependent: :delete_all`

### 3. Background Job: `ContainerMetricsJob`
Every 5 min (via `config/recurring.yml`). For each running sandbox:
- Fetch `Docker::Container.get(container_id).stats(stream: false)`
- Extract CPU % and memory MB (reuse existing `calculate_cpu_percent` logic — extract to a module `StatsCalculator`)
- Insert `ContainerMetric` record
- Purge records older than 1 hour: `ContainerMetric.where("recorded_at < ?", 1.hour.ago).delete_all`

**Files:**
- `app/jobs/container_metrics_job.rb`
- `config/recurring.yml` — add `collect_container_metrics` entry
- `app/services/stats_calculator.rb` — extract shared CPU calc (used by job + DashboardController + Admin)

### 4. Metrics JSON endpoint
Returns persisted history for initial page load.

```ruby
# GET /sandboxes/:id/metrics.json
def metrics
  points = @sandbox.container_metrics.recent
  render json: points.map { |m| { t: m.recorded_at.to_i, cpu: m.cpu_percent, mem: m.memory_mb.round(0) } }
end
```

**Files:**
- `app/controllers/sandboxes_controller.rb` — add `metrics` action
- `config/routes.rb` — add `get :metrics, on: :member`
- `app/policies/sandbox_policy.rb` — add `metrics?`

### 5. Live stats: reuse existing endpoint
The existing `DashboardController#stats` already returns live Docker stats. The Stimulus controller will poll `GET /sandboxes/:id/stats.json` every 5s for the current CPU/MEM values and append them to the in-memory data array.

**Edit:** `app/controllers/dashboard_controller.rb` — add `respond_to` block so it can return JSON (currently renders a partial). OR: add a `stats` action to `SandboxesController` that returns JSON. Simpler: add to SandboxesController.

**File:** `app/controllers/sandboxes_controller.rb` — add `stats` action returning JSON `{ cpu: X, mem: Y }`

### 6. Stimulus sparkline controller
`app/javascript/controllers/sparkline_controller.js`

**Behavior:**
1. `connect()`: fetch `/sandboxes/:id/metrics.json` → populate `this.cpuData` and `this.memData` arrays
2. Start polling `/sandboxes/:id/stats.json` every 5s → append new point with current timestamp
3. Keep only last 30 min of data points (trim old entries)
4. After each update, redraw two `<svg>` elements with `<polyline>` sparklines
5. Show current value as text label next to each sparkline
6. `disconnect()`: clear polling interval

**SVG rendering:**
- Each sparkline: ~200×36px, no axes, just the line + subtle fill
- CPU: fixed Y scale 0–100%
- MEM: auto-scaled Y based on max value in window
- Tailwind classes for colors: blue for CPU, purple for MEM

### 7. Show page update
`app/views/sandboxes/show.html.erb` — add between Actions and Routes (only for running sandboxes):

```erb
<% if @sandbox.status == "running" %>
  <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 px-5 py-3"
       data-controller="sparkline"
       data-sparkline-metrics-url-value="<%= metrics_sandbox_path(@sandbox, format: :json) %>"
       data-sparkline-stats-url-value="<%= stats_sandbox_path(@sandbox, format: :json) %>">
    <div class="flex items-center gap-6">
      <div class="flex-1">
        <span class="text-xs text-gray-500">CPU</span>
        <span class="text-xs font-mono font-medium" data-sparkline-target="cpuValue">—</span>
        <div data-sparkline-target="cpuChart"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="flex-1">
        <span class="text-xs text-gray-500">MEM</span>
        <span class="text-xs font-mono font-medium" data-sparkline-target="memValue">—</span>
        <div data-sparkline-target="memChart"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
<% end %>
```

### 8. Refactor: extract `StatsCalculator`
Move `calculate_cpu_percent` out of DashboardController and Admin::SandboxesController into a shared module.

**File:** `app/services/stats_calculator.rb`
```ruby
module StatsCalculator
  def self.cpu_percent(stats) ... end
  def self.memory_mb(stats) ... end
end
```
Update DashboardController and Admin::SandboxesController to use it.

```
    93→    end
    94→
    95→    user = Current.user

    97→
    98→    @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
    99→    SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id, archive: archive)
   100→
   101→    notice = archive ? "Archiving sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." : "Destroying sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..."
   102→    respond_to do |format|
   103→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: notice }
   104→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
   105→    end
   106→  end
   107→
   108→  def archive_restore
   109→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
   110→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
   111→      return
   112→    end
   113→
   114→    @sandbox.start_job("restoring")
   115→    SandboxRestoreJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   116→    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Restoring sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..."
   117→  end
   118→
   119→  def start
   120→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
   121→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
   122→      return
   123→    end
   124→
   125→    @sandbox.start_job("starting")
   126→    SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   127→
   128→    respond_to do |format|
   129→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Starting sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
   130→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
   131→    end
   132→  end
   133→
   134→  def stop
   135→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
   136→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
   137→      return
   138→    end
   139→
   140→    @sandbox.start_job("stopping")
   141→    SandboxStopJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   142→
   143→    respond_to do |format|
   144→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Stopping sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
   145→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
   146→    end
   147→  end
   148→
   149→  def retry
   150→    return unless @sandbox.job_failed?
   151→
   152→    @sandbox.update!(job_error: nil)
   153→
   154→    case @sandbox.status
   155→    when "destroyed", "pending"
   156→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Cannot retry creation. Please create a new sandbox."
   157→    when "stopped"
   158→      SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   159→      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Retrying start..."
   160→    when "running"
   161→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Sandbox is already running"
   162→    end
   163→  end
   164→
   165→  private
   166→

   169→    authorize @sandbox
   170→  end
   171→
   172→  def set_archived_sandbox
   173→    @sandbox = Current.user.sandboxes.archived.find(params[:id])
   174→    authorize @sandbox, :archive_restore?
   175→  end
   176→end
```

```
     1→class DashboardController < ApplicationController
     2→  def index
     3→    @sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).order(:name)
     4→    @archived_sandboxes = Current.user.sandboxes.archived.includes(:routes).order(:name)
     5→    @vnc_active_ids = running_vnc_ids
     6→    @tailscale_ips = tailscale_ips_for(@sandboxes)
     7→  end
     8→
     9→  def card
    10→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).find(params[:id])
    11→    authorize sandbox
    12→    vnc_active = VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)
    13→    tailscale_ip = sandbox.tailscale? ? TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox) : nil
    14→    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(helpers.dom_id(sandbox), partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: sandbox, vnc_active: vnc_active, tailscale_ip: tailscale_ip })
    15→  end
    16→
    17→  def stats
    18→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
    19→    authorize sandbox, :stats?
    20→
    21→    if sandbox.status == "running" && sandbox.container_id.present?
    22→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
    23→      raw = container.stats(stream: false)
    24→
    25→      # Handle case where container is shutting down and returns nil/incomplete stats
    26→      if raw.present?
    27→        networks = raw["networks"] || {}
    28→        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["rx_bytes"] || 0 }
    29→        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["tx_bytes"] || 0 }
    30→
    31→        blkio = raw.dig("blkio_stats", "io_service_bytes_recursive") || []
    32→        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "read" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    33→        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "write" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    34→
    35→        @stats = {
    36→          cpu_percent: calculate_cpu_percent(raw),
    37→          memory_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "usage") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    38→          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    39→          net_rx: net_rx,
    40→          net_tx: net_tx,
    41→          disk_read: disk_read,
    42→          disk_write: disk_write,
    43→          pids: raw.dig("pids_stats", "current") || 0
    44→        }
    45→      end
    46→    end
    47→
    48→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: @stats, sandbox: sandbox }
    49→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    50→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: nil }
    51→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    52→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: sandbox }
    53→  end
    54→
    55→  private
    56→
    57→  def running_vnc_ids
    58→    Dir.glob(File.join(VncManager::DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-*.yml")).filter_map { |f|
    59→      File.basename(f).match(/\Avnc-(\d+)\.yml\z/)&.[](1)&.to_i
    60→    }.to_set
    61→  rescue
    62→    Set.new
    63→  end
    64→
    65→  def tailscale_ips_for(sandboxes)
    66→    ts_sandboxes = sandboxes.select { |s| s.tailscale? && s.container_id.present? }
    67→    return {} if ts_sandboxes.empty?
    68→
    69→    all_containers = Docker::Container.all
    70→    container_map = all_containers.each_with_object({}) { |c, h| h[c.id] = c }
    71→
    72→    ts_sandboxes.each_with_object({}) do |sandbox, ips|
    73→      container = container_map[sandbox.container_id]
    74→      next unless container
    75→      network = sandbox.user.tailscale_network
    76→      next unless network.present?
    77→      ip = container.info.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks", network, "IPAddress")
    78→      ips[sandbox.id] = ip if ip.present?
    79→    end
    80→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    81→    {}
    82→  end
    83→
    84→  def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)
    85→    cpu_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f -
    86→                stats.dig("precpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f
    87→    system_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f -
    88→                   stats.dig("precpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f
    89→    num_cpus = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "online_cpus") || 1
    90→
    91→    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?
    92→    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)
    93→  end
    94→end
    95→
```

```
   137→                  <span class="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-purple-100 text-purple-800">TCP</span>
   138→                <% end %>

   141→                <% if route.http? %>
   142→                  <a href="<%= route.url %>" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="hover:text-blue-600"><%= route.domain %></a>
   143→                <% else %>
   144→                  <span class="text-purple-700">:<%= route.public_port %></span>
   145→                <% end %>
   146→              </td>
   147→              <td class="px-6 py-3 text-gray-600"><%= route.port %></td>
   148→              <td class="px-6 py-3 text-right">
   149→                <%= button_to "Remove",
   150→                      sandbox_route_path(@sandbox, route),
   151→                      method: :delete,
   152→                      data: { confirm: route.http? ? "Remove route #{route.domain}?" : "Remove TCP route :#{route.public_port}?" },
   153→                      class: "text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 text-xs bg-transparent border-0 cursor-pointer p-0" %>
   154→              </td>
   155→            </tr>
   156→          <% end %>
   157→        </tbody>
   158→      </table>
   159→    <% else %>

   162→      </div>

   203→  <%# Snapshots %>
   204→  <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
   205→    <div class="px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between">
   206→      <h2 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-900">Snapshots</h2>
   207→      <button
   208→        onclick="(function(){var d=new Date();var ts='snap-'+d.getFullYear()+(String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'))+(String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0'))+'-'+(String(d.getHours()).padStart(2,'0'))+(String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0'))+(String(d.getSeconds()).padStart(2,'0'));document.getElementById('show-snap-name').value=ts;document.getElementById('create-snapshot-modal').classList.remove('hidden');document.getElementById('show-snap-name').select();})()"
   209→        class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700">
   210→        + Create Snapshot
   211→      </button>
   212→    </div>

   214→    <% if @sandbox_snapshots.empty? %>

   217→      </div>

   219→      <table class="w-full text-sm">

   221→          <tr>
   222→            <th class="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Name</th>
   223→            <th class="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Layers</th>

   231→            <tr>

   233→                <%= snap[:name] %>
   234→                <% if snap[:label].present? %>
   235→                  <span class="block text-xs text-gray-500 font-sans font-normal"><%= snap[:label] %></span>
   236→                <% end %>
   237→              </td>
   238→              <td class="px-6 py-3 text-gray-600">
   239→                <% (snap[:layers] || []).each do |layer| %>
   240→                  <span class="inline-block px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 text-xs rounded mr-1"><%= layer[0].upcase %></span>
   241→                <% end %>
   242→              </td>
   243→              <td class="px-6 py-3 text-gray-600">
   244→                <%= number_to_human_size(snap[:total_size] || 0) %>
   245→              </td>
   246→              <td class="px-6 py-3 text-gray-500 text-xs">
   247→                <%= snap[:created_at]&.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") %>
   248→              </td>

   250→                <div class="flex items-center gap-3 justify-end">
   251→                  <%= link_to "New sandbox", new_sandbox_path(snapshot: snap[:name]),
   252→                        class: "text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 text-xs" %>
   253→                  <%= button_to "Delete",
   254→                        snapshot_path(snap[:name]),
   255→                        method: :delete,

   257→                        class: "text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 text-xs bg-transparent border-0 cursor-pointer p-0" %>
   258→                </div>
   259→              </td>
   260→            </tr>
   261→          <% end %>
   262→        </tbody>
   263→      </table>
   264→    <% end %>
   265→  </div>
   266→</div>
   267→
   268→<%# Create Snapshot Modal %>
   269→<div id="create-snapshot-modal" class="hidden fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center z-50" onclick="if(event.target===this)this.classList.add('hidden')">
   270→  <div class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl w-full max-w-md mx-4">
   271→    <div class="px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between">
   272→      <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Create Snapshot</h3>

   274→    </div>

   276→    <%= form_with url: snapshot_sandbox_path(@sandbox), method: :post, class: "px-6 py-4 space-y-4" do |f| %>

   279→              required: true,

   283→      </div>

   285→      <div>

   298→          <% if @sandbox.data_path.present? && @btrfs %>
   299→            <label class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">

   301→              Data directory (<%= @sandbox.data_path %>)
   302→            </label>
   303→          <% end %>

   307→              Workspace volume

   309→          <% end %>
   310→          <% unless @btrfs %>

   312→              BTRFS not detected — only the container layer is available.

   314→          <% end %>
   315→        </div>
   316→      </div>
   317→
   318→      <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-2">

   320→                class="px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-700 hover:text-gray-900">Cancel</button>
   321→        <%= f.submit "Create Snapshot",
   322→              class: "px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 cursor-pointer" %>
   323→      </div>
   324→    <% end %>
   325→  </div>
   326→</div>
```

```
     1→class SandboxPolicy < ApplicationPolicy
     2→  def index?                 = true
     3→  def show?                  = owner_only?
     4→  def create?                = true

     6→  def destroy?               = owner_or_admin?
     7→  def start?                 = owner_or_admin?

     9→  def retry?                 = owner_only?
    10→  def logs?                  = owner_or_admin?

    12→  def card?                  = owner_only?

    14→  def snapshot?              = owner_only?
    15→  def restore?               = owner_only?
    16→  def archive_restore?       = owner_or_admin?

    19→  def tailscale_disconnect?  = owner_only?

    21→  private
    22→
    23→  def owner_only?
    24→    record.user_id == user.id
    25→  end
    26→
    27→  def owner_or_admin?
    28→    admin? || record.user_id == user.id
    29→  end
    30→
    31→  class Scope < ApplicationPolicy::Scope
    32→    def resolve
    33→      scope.where(user:).active
    34→    end
    35→  end
    36→end
```

```
     1→module Admin
     2→  class SandboxesController < BaseController
     3→    before_action :set_sandbox, except: [ :stats, :archive_restore, :purge ]
     4→    before_action :set_archived_sandbox, only: [ :archive_restore, :purge ]
     5→
     6→    def destroy
     7→      authorize @sandbox
     8→      if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
     9→        redirect_to admin_dashboard_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    10→        return
    11→      end
    12→
    13→      @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
    14→      SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    15→
    16→      respond_to do |format|
    17→        format.html { redirect_to admin_dashboard_path, notice: "Destroying sandbox #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    18→        format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "admin/dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    19→      end
    20→    end
    21→
    22→    def start
    23→      authorize @sandbox
    24→      if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    25→        redirect_to admin_dashboard_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    26→        return
    27→      end
    28→
    29→      @sandbox.start_job("starting")
    30→      SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    31→
    32→      respond_to do |format|
    33→        format.html { redirect_to admin_dashboard_path, notice: "Starting sandbox #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    34→        format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "admin/dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    35→      end
    36→    end
    37→
    38→    def stop
    39→      authorize @sandbox
    40→      if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    41→        redirect_to admin_dashboard_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    42→        return
    43→      end
    44→
    45→      @sandbox.start_job("stopping")
    46→      SandboxStopJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    47→
    48→      respond_to do |format|
    49→        format.html { redirect_to admin_dashboard_path, notice: "Stopping sandbox #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    50→        format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "admin/dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    51→      end
    52→    end
    53→
    54→    def archive_restore
    55→      authorize @sandbox, :archive_restore?
    56→      @sandbox.start_job("restoring")
    57→      SandboxRestoreJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    58→      redirect_to admin_dashboard_path, notice: "Restoring sandbox #{@sandbox.name}..."
    59→    end
    60→
    61→    def purge
    62→      authorize @sandbox, :purge?
    63→      @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
    64→      SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id, archive: false)
    65→      redirect_to admin_dashboard_path, notice: "Purging sandbox #{@sandbox.name}..."
    66→    end
    67→
    68→    def stats
    69→      @sandbox = Sandbox.find(params[:id])
    70→      authorize @sandbox
    71→      if @sandbox.status == "running" && @sandbox.container_id.present?
    72→        container = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id)
    73→        raw = container.stats(stream: false)
    74→
    75→        networks = raw["networks"] || {}
    76→        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["rx_bytes"] || 0 }
    77→        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["tx_bytes"] || 0 }
    78→
    79→        blkio = raw.dig("blkio_stats", "io_service_bytes_recursive") || []
    80→        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "read" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    81→        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "write" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    82→
    83→        @stats = {
    84→          cpu_percent: calculate_cpu_percent(raw),
    85→          memory_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "usage") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    86→          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    87→          net_rx: net_rx,
    88→          net_tx: net_tx,
    89→          disk_read: disk_read,
    90→          disk_write: disk_write,
    91→          pids: raw.dig("pids_stats", "current") || 0
    92→        }
    93→      end
    94→
    95→      render partial: "admin/dashboard/sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: @stats, sandbox: @sandbox }
    96→    rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    97→      render partial: "admin/dashboard/sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: nil }
    98→    rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    99→      render partial: "admin/dashboard/sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: @sandbox }
   100→    end
   101→
   102→    private
   103→
   104→    def set_sandbox
   105→      @sandbox = Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])
   106→    end
   107→
   108→    def set_archived_sandbox
   109→      @sandbox = Sandbox.archived.find(params[:id])
   110→    end
   111→
   112→    def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)
   113→      cpu_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f -
   114→                  stats.dig("precpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f
   115→      system_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f -
   116→                     stats.dig("precpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f
   117→      num_cpus = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "online_cpus") || 1
   118→
   119→      return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?
   120→      ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)
   121→    end
   122→  end
   123→end
   124→
```

```json
  "content": "module StatsCalculator\n  def self.cpu_percent(stats)\n    cpu_delta = stats.dig(\"cpu_stats\", \"cpu_usage\", \"total_usage\").to_f -\n                stats.dig(\"precpu_stats\", \"cpu_usage\", \"total_usage\").to_f\n    system_delta = stats.dig(\"cpu_stats\", \"system_cpu_usage\").to_f -\n                   stats.dig(\"precpu_stats\", \"system_cpu_usage\").to_f\n    num_cpus = stats.dig(\"cpu_stats\", \"online_cpus\") || 1\n\n    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?\n    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)\n  end\n\n  def self.memory_mb(stats)\n    (stats.dig(\"memory_stats\", \"usage\") || 0) / 1_048_576.0\n  end\nend\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/jobs/container_metrics_job.rb",
  "content": "class ContainerMetricsJob < ApplicationJob\n  queue_as :default\n\n  def perform\n    now = Time.current\n\n    Sandbox.running.where.not(container_id: nil).find_each do |sandbox|\n      raw = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id).stats(stream: false)\n      next if raw.blank?\n\n      ContainerMetric.create!(\n        sandbox: sandbox,\n        cpu_percent: StatsCalculator.cpu_percent(raw),\n        memory_mb: StatsCalculator.memory_mb(raw),\n        recorded_at: now\n      )\n    rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n      Rails.logger.warn(\"ContainerMetricsJob: #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}\")\n    end\n\n    ContainerMetric.where(\"recorded_at < ?\", 1.hour.ago).delete_all\n  end\nend\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/javascript/controllers/sparkline_controller.js",
  "content": "import { Controller } from \"@hotwired/stimulus\"\n\nexport default class extends Controller {\n  static values = { metricsUrl: String, statsUrl: String }\n  static targets = [\"cpuChart\", \"memChart\", \"cpuValue\", \"memValue\"]\n\n  connect() {\n    this.cpuData = []\n    this.memData = []\n    this.windowMs = 30 * 60 * 1000\n\n    this.loadHistory()\n    this.pollInterval = setInterval(() => this.pollStats(), 5000)\n  }\n\n  disconnect() {\n    if (this.pollInterval) clearInterval(this.pollInterval)\n  }\n\n  async loadHistory() {\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch(this.metricsUrlValue)\n      if (!resp.ok) return\n      const points = await resp.json()\n      points.forEach(p => {\n        this.cpuData.push({ t: p.t * 1000, v: p.cpu })\n        this.memData.push({ t: p.t * 1000, v: p.mem })\n      })\n      this.render()\n    } catch (e) {\n      // ignore\n    }\n  }\n\n  async pollStats() {\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch(this.statsUrlValue)\n      if (!resp.ok) return\n      const data = await resp.json()\n      if (data.cpu == null) return\n      const now = Date.now()\n      this.cpuData.push({ t: now, v: data.cpu })\n      this.memData.push({ t: now, v: data.mem })\n      this.trim()\n      this.render()\n    } catch (e) {\n      // ignore\n    }\n  }\n\n  trim() {\n    const cutoff = Date.now() - this.windowMs\n    this.cpuData = this.cpuData.filter(p => p.t > cutoff)\n    this.memData = this.memData.filter(p => p.t > cutoff)\n  }\n\n  render() {\n    this.renderChart(this.cpuChartTarget, this.cpuData, 0, 100)\n    this.renderChart(this.memChartTarget, this.memData, 0, null)\n\n    const lastCpu = this.cpuData[this.cpuData.length - 1]\n    const lastMem = this.memData[this.memData.length - 1]\n    if (lastCpu) this.cpuValueTarget.textContent = `${lastCpu.v.toFixed(1)}%`\n    if (lastMem) this.memValueTarget.textContent = `${Math.round(lastMem.v)} MB`\n  }\n\n  renderChart(target, data, fixedMin, fixedMax) {\n    if (data.length < 2) {\n      target.innerHTML = \"\"\n      return\n    }\n\n    const w = 200, h = 36, pad = 1\n    const min = fixedMin != null ? fixedMin : Math.min(...data.map(p => p.v))\n    let max = fixedMax != null ? fixedMax : Math.max(...data.map(p => p.v))\n    if (max === min) max = min + 1\n\n    const tMin = data[0].t, tMax = data[data.length - 1].t\n    const tRange = tMax - tMin || 1\n\n    const points = data.map(p => {\n      const x = pad + ((p.t - tMin) / tRange) * (w - 2 * pad)\n      const y = h - pad - ((p.v - min) / (max - min)) * (h - 2 * pad)\n      return `${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`\n    }).join(\" \")\n\n    const lastPoint = data[data.length - 1]\n    const lastX = pad + ((lastPoint.t - tMin) / tRange) * (w - 2 * pad)\n    const lastY = h - pad - ((lastPoint.v - min) / (max - min)) * (h - 2 * pad)\n\n    const fillPoints = `${pad},${h} ${points} ${lastX.toFixed(1)},${h}`\n\n    const isBlue = fixedMax === 100\n    const strokeColor = isBlue ? \"#3b82f6\" : \"#8b5cf6\"\n    const fillColor = isBlue ? \"rgba(59,130,246,0.1)\" : \"rgba(139,92,246,0.1)\"\n\n    target.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox=\"0 0 ${w} ${h}\" class=\"w-full h-9\" preserveAspectRatio=\"none\">\n      <polygon points=\"${fillPoints}\" fill=\"${fillColor}\" />\n      <polyline points=\"${points}\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"${strokeColor}\" stroke-width=\"1.5\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" />\n      <circle cx=\"${lastX.toFixed(1)}\" cy=\"${lastY.toFixed(1)}\" r=\"2\" fill=\"${strokeColor}\" />\n    </svg>`\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "  def tailscale_ips_for(sandboxes)\n    ts_sandboxes = sandboxes.select { |s| s.tailscale? && s.container_id.present? }\n    return {} if ts_sandboxes.empty?\n\n    all_containers = Docker::Container.all\n    container_map = all_containers.each_with_object({}) { |c, h| h[c.id] = c }\n\n    ts_sandboxes.each_with_object({}) do |sandbox, ips|\n      container = container_map[sandbox.container_id]\n      next unless container\n      network = sandbox.user.tailscale_network\n      next unless network.present?\n      ip = container.info.dig(\"NetworkSettings\", \"Networks\", network, \"IPAddress\")\n      ips[sandbox.id] = ip if ip.present?\n    end\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError\n    {}\n  end\n\n  def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)\n    cpu_delta = stats.dig(\"cpu_stats\", \"cpu_usage\", \"total_usage\").to_f -\n                stats.dig(\"precpu_stats\", \"cpu_usage\", \"total_usage\").to_f\n    system_delta = stats.dig(\"cpu_stats\", \"system_cpu_usage\").to_f -\n                   stats.dig(\"precpu_stats\", \"system_cpu_usage\").to_f\n    num_cpus = stats.dig(\"cpu_stats\", \"online_cpus\") || 1\n\n    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?\n    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)\n  end\nend",
  "new_string": "  def tailscale_ips_for(sandboxes)\n    ts_sandboxes = sandboxes.select { |s| s.tailscale? && s.container_id.present? }\n    return {} if ts_sandboxes.empty?\n\n    all_containers = Docker::Container.all\n    container_map = all_containers.each_with_object({}) { |c, h| h[c.id] = c }\n\n    ts_sandboxes.each_with_object({}) do |sandbox, ips|\n      container = container_map[sandbox.container_id]\n      next unless container\n      network = sandbox.user.tailscale_network\n      next unless network.present?\n      ip = container.info.dig(\"NetworkSettings\", \"Networks\", network, \"IPAddress\")\n      ips[sandbox.id] = ip if ip.present?\n    end\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError\n    {}\n  end\nend"
```

> AGENT

All 13 files from the plan are accounted for. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

**New files (5):**
- `db/migrate/*_create_container_metrics.rb` — Migration with `sandbox_id`, `cpu_percent`, `memory_mb`, `recorded_at` + composite index
- `app/models/container_metric.rb` — Model with `recent` scope (last 30 min)
- `app/services/stats_calculator.rb` — Extracted `cpu_percent` and `memory_mb` calculations
- `app/jobs/container_metrics_job.rb` — Collects metrics every 5 min, purges >1 hour old
- `app/javascript/controllers/sparkline_controller.js` — Stimulus controller rendering SVG sparklines with live polling

**Edited files (8):**
- `app/models/sandbox.rb` — Added `has_many :container_metrics, dependent: :delete_all`
- `app/policies/sandbox_policy.rb` — Added `metrics?` policy
- `config/recurring.yml` — Added `collect_container_metrics` every 5 min
- `config/routes.rb` — Added `get :metrics` member route
- `app/controllers/sandboxes_controller.rb` — Added `metrics` action, updated `before_action`
- `app/controllers/dashboard_controller.rb` — Uses `StatsCalculator`, added JSON `respond_to` for stats, removed private `calculate_cpu_percent`
- `app/controllers/admin/sandboxes_controller.rb` — Uses `StatsCalculator`, removed private `calculate_cpu_percent`
- `app/views/sandboxes/show.html.erb` — Added sparkline section between Actions and Routes for running sandboxes

**How it works:**
1. Background job collects CPU/MEM every 5 min → persisted in `container_metrics`
2. Show page loads history from `GET /sandboxes/:id/metrics.json`
3. Polls `GET /sandboxes/:id/stats.json` every 5s for live data points
4. Renders two SVG sparklines (blue=CPU 0-100%, purple=MEM auto-scaled) with current value labels

     1→# LOCAL.md
     2→
     3→Local development environment guide for Sandcastle. Use this file when debugging locally with Docker Compose.
     4→
     5→## Overview
     6→
     7→Sandcastle has two local deployment modes:
     8→
     9→1. **`mise run deploy:local`** — Production-like environment (builds production image, uses `docker-compose.local.yml`)
    10→2. **`mise run deploy:dev`** — Development environment with live reload (mounts source code, uses `docker-compose.dev.yml`)
    11→
    12→Both modes run the full stack (Traefik, PostgreSQL, Rails web, Solid Queue worker) in Docker containers.
    13→
    14→## Prerequisites
    15→
    16→- Docker with Sysbox runtime installed
    17→- `mise` installed (`brew install mise`)
    18→- `sandcastle-web` network created: `docker network create sandcastle-web`
    19→- Self-signed certificates (auto-generated on first run or created manually)
    20→
    21→## Quick Start
    22→
    23→### Production-like Local Environment
    24→
    25→```bash
    26→# Start (builds image and runs)
    27→mise run deploy:local
    28→
    29→# Access at:
    30→#   - https://sandcastle.local:8443 (via Traefik, recommended)
    31→#   - http://sandcastle.local:8080 (via Traefik)
    32→
    33→# Stop
    34→mise run deploy:local:down
    35→
    36→# Reset (remove all volumes and containers, rebuild)
    37→mise run deploy:local:reset
    38→
    39→# View logs
    40→mise run deploy:local:logs
    41→```
    42→
    43→### Development Environment (Live Reload)
    44→
    45→```bash
    46→# Start (mounts source code)
    47→mise run deploy:dev
    48→
    49→# Access at:
    50→#   - https://localhost:8443
    51→#   - http://localhost:8080
    52→
    53→# Stop
    54→mise run deploy:dev:down
    55→
    56→# Reset
    57→mise run deploy:dev:reset
    58→```
    59→
    60→## Commands
    61→
    62→### Starting the Environment
    63→
    64→```bash
    65→# Production-like (full build)
    66→mise run deploy:local
    67→
    68→# Development (live reload)
    69→mise run deploy:dev
    70→```
    71→
    72→### Stopping
    73→
    74→```bash
    75→# Production-like
    76→mise run deploy:local:down
    77→
    78→# Development
    79→mise run deploy:dev:down
    80→
    81→# Alternative: stop all containers manually
    82→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml down
    83→docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml down
    84→```
    85→
    86→### Resetting (Clean Slate)
    87→
    88→The reset commands remove WeTTY containers, Tailscale sidecars, all volumes, and orphaned containers:
    89→
    90→```bash
    91→# Production-like
    92→mise run deploy:local:reset
    93→
    94→# Development
    95→mise run deploy:dev:reset
    96→```
    97→
    98→This is useful when:
    99→- Database is in a bad state
   100→- Volumes are corrupted
   101→- You want a fresh start
   102→
   103→**Warning:** This deletes all data including database, user sandboxes, and Traefik config.
   104→
   105→### Viewing Logs
   106→
   107→```bash
   108→# Tail all logs (production-like)
   109→mise run deploy:local:logs
   110→
   111→# Tail all logs (development)
   112→docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml logs -f
   113→
   114→# Specific service
   115→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml logs -f web
   116→docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml logs -f worker
   117→
   118→# Last 100 lines
   119→docker logs sandcastle-web --tail 100
   120→
   121→# Follow logs for a specific container
   122→docker logs -f sandcastle-web
   123→docker logs -f sandcastle-worker
   124→```
   125→
   126→## Inspecting the Environment
   127→
   128→### Container Status
   129→
   130→```bash
   131→# List all Sandcastle containers
   132→docker ps -a | grep sandcastle
   133→
   134→# List all containers (including sandboxes, WeTTY, Tailscale)
   135→docker ps -a
   136→
   137→# Filter by name pattern
   138→docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-wetty-"
   139→docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-ts-"
   140→```
   141→
   142→### Database Access
   143→
   144→```bash
   145→# Enter PostgreSQL container
   146→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production
   147→
   148→# Or for dev environment
   149→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_development
   150→
   151→# Run a query
   152→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production -c "SELECT * FROM users;"
   153→
   154→# Check database size
   155→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production -c "\l+"
   156→```
   157→
   158→### Rails Console
   159→
   160→```bash
   161→# Production-like environment
   162→docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails console
   163→
   164→# Development environment (same command)
   165→docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails console
   166→```
   167→
   168→### Inspect Volumes
   169→
   170→```bash
   171→# List volumes
   172→docker volume ls | grep sandcastle
   173→
   174→# Inspect a volume
   175→docker volume inspect sandcastle_sandcastle-data
   176→docker volume inspect sandcastle_pgdata
   177→
   178→# Browse volume contents (via temporary container)
   179→docker run --rm -v sandcastle_sandcastle-data:/data -it busybox sh
   180→# Inside container: ls -la /data
   181→```
   182→
   183→### Network Inspection
   184→
   185→```bash
   186→# List networks
   187→docker network ls | grep sandcastle
   188→
   189→# Inspect the bridge network
   190→docker network inspect sandcastle-web
   191→
   192→# Check what containers are on the network
   193→docker network inspect sandcastle-web -f '{{range .Containers}}{{.Name}} {{end}}'
   194→```
   195→
   196→### Traefik Dashboard
   197→
   198→Traefik doesn't expose the dashboard by default. To view routing config:
   199→
   200→```bash
   201→# Inspect Traefik dynamic config
   202→docker exec -it sandcastle_traefik_1 cat /data/dynamic/rails.yml
   203→
   204→# View all dynamic configs
   205→docker exec -it sandcastle_traefik_1 ls -la /data/dynamic/
   206→```
   207→
   208→### Docker Socket Access
   209→
   210→The web and worker containers need Docker socket access to manage sandbox containers:
   211→
   212→```bash
   213→# Check if socket is mounted
   214→docker inspect sandcastle-web | grep -A 5 Mounts | grep docker.sock
   215→
   216→# Test Docker access from inside the container
   217→docker exec sandcastle-web docker ps
   218→```
   219→
   220→## Environment Variables
   221→
   222→### Production-like (`docker-compose.local.yml`)
   223→
   224→| Variable | Default | Description |
   225→|----------|---------|-------------|
   226→| `BUILD_VERSION` | `dev` | Version tag (auto-set from git) |
   227→| `BUILD_GIT_SHA` | `local` | Git commit SHA (auto-set) |
   228→| `BUILD_GIT_DIRTY` | `false` | Whether working tree is dirty |
   229→| `BUILD_DATE` | current | Build timestamp |
   230→| `SECRET_KEY_BASE` | `deadbeef` | Rails secret (change for production) |
   231→| `DB_PASSWORD` | `sandcastle` | PostgreSQL password |
   232→| `DOCKER_GID` | `0` | Docker socket group ID |
   233→
   234→### Development (`docker-compose.dev.yml`)
   235→
   236→Same as production-like, plus:
   237→
   238→| Variable | Default | Description |
   239→|----------|---------|-------------|
   240→| `RAILS_ENV` | `development` | Rails environment |
   241→| `SANDCASTLE_HOST` | `localhost` | Host for URLs |
   242→
   243→## Architecture
   244→
   245→### Services
   246→
   247→- **traefik** — Reverse proxy (ports 8080:80, 8443:443)
   248→- **postgres** — PostgreSQL 18 database
   249→- **web** — Rails app (Puma server on port 80 inside container)
   250→- **worker** — Solid Queue background worker
   251→- **migrate** — One-shot migration runner (runs on startup)
   252→- **init-data** — One-shot data directory initializer
   253→- **init-traefik** — One-shot Traefik config initializer
   254→
   255→### Networks
   256→
   257→- **sandcastle-web** — External bridge network (must be created manually)
   258→- **default** — Default compose network for internal communication
   259→
   260→### Volumes
   261→
   262→| Volume | Purpose |
   263→|--------|---------|
   264→| `pgdata` | PostgreSQL data |
   265→| `sandcastle-data` | User homes, sandbox volumes, Tailscale state |
   266→| `traefik-data` | Traefik config and certificates |
   267→| `bundle-data` | Ruby gems (dev mode only) |
   268→| `node-modules` | Node packages (dev mode only) |
   269→
   270→### Ports
   271→
   272→- **8080** — HTTP (Traefik web entrypoint)
   273→- **8443** — HTTPS (Traefik websecure entrypoint)
   274→- **2201-2299** — SSH ports for sandboxes (mapped to host)
   275→
   276→## Troubleshooting
   277→
   278→### Containers Won't Start
   279→
   280→```bash
   281→# Check container logs
   282→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml logs
   283→
   284→# Inspect failed container
   285→docker inspect sandcastle-web
   286→
   287→# Check for port conflicts
   288→lsof -i :8080
   289→lsof -i :8443
   290→```
   291→
   292→### Database Connection Errors
   293→
   294→```bash
   295→# Check if postgres is healthy
   296→docker inspect sandcastle_postgres_1 | grep Health -A 10
   297→
   298→# Check database logs
   299→docker logs sandcastle_postgres_1
   300→
   301→# Try connecting manually
   302→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production
   303→```
   304→
   305→### Docker Socket Permission Denied
   306→
   307→The web/worker containers need access to `/var/run/docker.sock`. On macOS this usually works, on Linux you may need to set `DOCKER_GID`:
   308→
   309→```bash
   310→# Find Docker socket GID
   311→stat -c '%g' /var/run/docker.sock  # Linux
   312→stat -f '%g' /var/run/docker.sock  # macOS
   313→
   314→# Set it in your environment
   315→export DOCKER_GID=988
   316→mise run deploy:local
   317→```
   318→
   319→### Traefik Not Routing
   320→
   321→```bash
   322→# Check if sandcastle-web network exists
   323→docker network ls | grep sandcastle-web
   324→
   325→# Create it if missing
   326→docker network create sandcastle-web
   327→
   328→# Restart Traefik
   329→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml restart traefik
   330→
   331→# Check Traefik logs
   332→docker logs sandcastle_traefik_1
   333→```
   334→
   335→### Sandbox Containers Won't Start
   336→
   337→Sandboxes require the Sysbox runtime:
   338→
   339→```bash
   340→# Check if Sysbox is installed
   341→docker info | grep -i sysbox
   342→
   343→# List available runtimes
   344→docker info | grep -A 5 Runtimes
   345→
   346→# Check Sysbox status (Linux)
   347→systemctl status sysbox
   348→```
   349→
   350→### Reset Everything
   351→
   352→If things are completely broken:
   353→
   354→```bash
   355→# Stop everything
   356→mise run deploy:local:down
   357→mise run deploy:dev:down
   358→
   359→# Remove all Sandcastle containers
   360→docker ps -a | grep -E "sandcastle|sc-wetty|sc-ts" | awk '{print $1}' | xargs -r docker rm -f
   361→
   362→# Remove all volumes
   363→docker volume ls | grep sandcastle | awk '{print $2}' | xargs -r docker volume rm
   364→
   365→# Remove network
   366→docker network rm sandcastle-web
   367→
   368→# Recreate network
   369→docker network create sandcastle-web
   370→
   371→# Start fresh
   372→mise run deploy:local:reset
   373→```
   374→
   375→## Default Admin Credentials
   376→
   377→The migrate service creates a default admin user (see `docker-compose.local.yml` lines 169-172):
   378→
   379→- **Username:** `thies`
   380→- **Email:** `thieso@gmail.com`
   381→- **Password:** `tubu`
   382→- **SSH Key:** (set in compose file)
   383→
   384→These are defined in the `SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_*` environment variables.
   385→
   386→## Development Workflow
   387→
   388→When using `mise run deploy:dev`:
   389→
   390→1. Source code is mounted at `/rails` in the container
   391→2. Changes to `.rb`, `.erb`, `.css` files reload automatically
   392→3. Tailwind CSS recompiles on save
   393→4. Gems are persisted in a volume (faster than bind mount)
   394→5. Database data persists between restarts
   395→
   396→**Tips:**
   397→- Edit files on the host, changes reflect immediately in the container
   398→- If you add a gem, rebuild: `docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml up --build`
   399→- Database migrations: `docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails db:migrate`
   400→- Run tests: `docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails test`
   401→
   402→## CLI Development
   403→
   404→The Go CLI is in `vendor/sandcastle-cli/`. It's not part of the Docker Compose setup:
   405→
   406→```bash
   407→cd vendor/sandcastle-cli
   408→
   409→# Build
   410→make build
   411→
   412→# Test against local Sandcastle
   413→export SANDCASTLE_API_URL=https://localhost:8443
   414→export SANDCASTLE_API_TOKEN=sc_...  # Get from UI
   415→./sandcastle list
   416→```
   417→
   418→## Useful Docker Commands
   419→
   420→```bash
   421→# Restart a single service
   422→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml restart web
   423→
   424→# Rebuild a single service
   425→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml up --build web
   426→
   427→# Execute a command in a container
   428→docker exec -it sandcastle-web bash
   429→
   430→# Copy files from container
   431→docker cp sandcastle-web:/rails/log/production.log ./
   432→
   433→# Copy files to container
   434→docker cp ./config.yml sandcastle-web:/rails/config/
   435→
   436→# Inspect container details
   437→docker inspect sandcastle-web
   438→
   439→# View resource usage
   440→docker stats
   441→
   442→# Prune unused images/volumes (be careful!)
   443→docker system prune -a
   444→```
   445→
   446→## File Locations
   447→
   448→### On Host
   449→
   450→- **Compose files:** `docker-compose.{local,dev}.yml`
   451→- **Mise config:** `mise.toml`
   452→- **Source code:** `.` (mounted in dev mode)
   453→
   454→### In Container
   455→
   456→- **Rails root:** `/rails`
   457→- **Data directory:** `/data`
   458→  - User homes: `/data/users/{username}/home`
   459→  - Sandbox volumes: `/data/sandboxes/{sandbox_name}/vol`
   460→  - Tailscale state: `/data/users/{username}/tailscale`
   461→  - WeTTY keys: `/data/wetty/{full_name}/`
   462→- **Traefik config:** `/data/traefik/dynamic/`
   463→- **Database:** `/var/lib/postgresql` (in postgres container)
   464→
   465→## Accessing Services
   466→
   467→| Service | URL | Notes |
   468→|---------|-----|-------|
   469→| Web UI (HTTPS) | https://sandcastle.local:8443 | Recommended, supports terminals |
   470→| Web UI (HTTP) | http://sandcastle.local:8080 | No TLS |
   471→| PostgreSQL | `localhost:5432` | Not exposed by default |
   472→| Traefik | — | No dashboard exposed |
   473→
   474→Add to `/etc/hosts` for `sandcastle.local`:
   475→```
   476→127.0.0.1 sandcastle.local
   477→```
   478→
   479→For localhost, use `https://localhost:8443`.
   480→
```

     1→# CLAUDE.md
     2→
     3→This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.
     4→
     5→## Project Overview
     6→
     7→Sandcastle is a self-hosted shared Docker sandbox platform. Users get isolated Sysbox containers with SSH access and a full Docker daemon inside. The stack is Rails 8.1 (Ruby 4.0) for the web/API backend and a Go CLI for user interaction.
     8→
     9→> **Important:** The container runtime is **Docker + Sysbox** (`sysbox-runc`). Incus/LXD is **not** used and should never be introduced. All container operations go through the `docker-api` gem via the Docker socket.
    10→
    11→## Commands
    12→
    13→### Rails App
    14→
    15→```bash
    16→bin/dev                              # Start dev server (web + Tailwind watcher via foreman)
    17→bin/rails test                       # Run all tests (Minitest)
    18→bin/rails test test/models/user_test.rb          # Run a single test file
    19→bin/rails test test/models/user_test.rb:10       # Run a single test by line
    20→bin/rails db:migrate                 # Run pending migrations
    21→bin/rails db:prepare                 # Migrate + seed
    22→bin/rubocop                          # Lint (rubocop-rails-omakase style)
    23→bin/brakeman                         # Security scan
    24→```
    25→
    26→### Go CLI (vendor/sandcastle-cli/)
    27→
    28→```bash
    29→cd vendor/sandcastle-cli && make build    # Build binary → ./sandcastle
    30→cd vendor/sandcastle-cli && go build ./...  # Quick compile check
    31→```
    32→
    33→Go module path is `github.com/sandcastle/cli`. When adding a new feature: add types to `api/types.go`, client methods to `api/client.go`, then the Cobra command in `cmd/`.
    34→
    35→### Patching Live
    36→
    37→To apply a single file change directly to the running production instance without a full redeploy:
    38→
    39→```bash
    40→scp path/to/file.rb sandcastle@sandman:/tmp/file.rb
    41→ssh sandcastle@sandman 'docker cp /tmp/file.rb sandcastle-web:/rails/path/to/file.rb && docker restart sandcastle-web'
    42→```
    43→
    44→### Full CI
    45→
    46→```bash
    47→bin/ci    # Runs rubocop → brakeman → bundler-audit → importmap audit → tests → system tests
    48→```
    49→
    50→### Releasing
    51→
    52→```bash
    53→mise run release          # Bump patch (v0.1.14 → v0.1.15), tag, push
    54→mise run release:minor    # Bump minor (v0.1.14 → v0.2.0), tag, push
    55→mise run release:major    # Bump major (v0.1.14 → v1.0.0), tag, push
    56→```
    57→
    58→Pushing a tag triggers the GitHub Actions `release.yml` workflow which:
    59→1. Builds CLI binaries via GoReleaser (cross-compiled for linux/darwin × amd64/arm64)
    60→2. Creates a GitHub release with signed/notarized archives
    61→3. Auto-updates the Homebrew formula in `thieso2/homebrew-tap` (requires `HOMEBREW_TAP_GITHUB_TOKEN` secret)
    62→
    63→CLI version is injected at build time via ldflags (`-X github.com/sandcastle/cli/cmd.Version`).
    64→
    65→## Architecture
    66→
    67→### BTRFS Support
    68→
    69→Sandcastle automatically detects and uses BTRFS subvolumes when the filesystem supports it:
    70→
    71→**Installer** (`setup_btrfs_subvolumes()` in `installer.sh`):
    72→- Detects if `$SANDCASTLE_HOME` is on BTRFS
    73→- Creates subvolumes for:
    74→  - `$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/users` (all user data)
    75→  - `$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/sandboxes` (sandbox volumes)
    76→  - `$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/postgres` (database)
    77→  - `$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/wetty` (terminal keys)
    78→  - `$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/traefik` (proxy config)
    79→  - `$DOCKYARD_ROOT/docker` (Docker data)
    80→
    81→**Rails** (`BtrfsHelper` service):
    82→- Creates per-user subvolumes: `/data/users/<username>`
    83→- Creates per-data-path subvolumes: `/data/users/<username>/data/<path>`
    84→- Falls back gracefully to regular directories if BTRFS operations fail
    85→- Uses `sudo -n /usr/bin/btrfs subvolume {show,create}` (restricted passwordless sudo)
    86→
    87→**Security**: Passwordless sudo limited to BTRFS subvolume commands only (`/etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle`).
    88→
    89→Benefits: efficient snapshots, better CoW performance, per-directory quotas.
    90→
    91→### Two Auth Systems
    92→
    93→- **Web UI**: Session-based via `Authentication` concern → cookie `session_id`
    94→- **API**: Bearer token via `ApiAuthentication` concern → `Authorization: Bearer sc_PREFIX_SECRET` header, bcrypt digest stored in `api_tokens` table. Token format: `sc_{8-char prefix}_{48-char secret}` (prefix stored plain, secret bcrypt-hashed)
    95→
    96→Both concerns provide `require_admin!` for admin-only actions.
    97→
    98→### Service Objects
    99→
   100→Business logic lives in plain Ruby service classes, not models or controllers:
   101→- `SandboxManager` — Container lifecycle (create/destroy/start/stop/snapshot/restore). Uses the `docker-api` gem to talk to the Docker daemon via socket.
   102→- `TerminalManager` — WeTTY web terminal sidecar lifecycle (see Web Terminal section below)
   103→- `TailscaleManager` — Per-user Tailscale sidecar lifecycle (see Tailscale section below)
   104→- `SystemStatus` — Docker daemon stats for admin dashboard
   105→
   106→All service errors inherit from `ServiceName::Error` and are rescued in `Api::BaseController` as 422 JSON responses. `ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound` maps to 404, `RecordInvalid` to 422.
   107→
   108→### Container Model
   109→
   110→Each sandbox = one Sysbox container (`sysbox-runc` runtime) with:
   111→- SSH on a unique host port (range 2201–2299)
   112→- User home bind-mounted from `/data/users/{name}/home`
   113→- Optional persistent volume at `/data/sandboxes/{name}/vol:/workspace`
   114→- Optional Tailscale connectivity via per-user bridge network + sidecar
   115→
   116→The sandbox image (`images/sandbox/`) is Ubuntu 24.04 with Docker-in-Docker, SSH server, and dev tools. Key details:
   117→- runc pinned to v1.1.15 (1.2+ fails in Sysbox)
   118→- Docker daemon MTU matches container's eth0 to avoid fragmentation
   119→- The `entrypoint.sh` creates the user, injects SSH keys, starts dockerd, then runs sshd
   120→
   121→### Web Terminal (WeTTY)
   122→
   123→Browser-based terminal access via [WeTTY](https://github.com/butlerx/wetty) sidecar containers. Each terminal open spawns an ephemeral WeTTY container that SSH's into the sandbox.
   124→
   125→**Architecture:**
   126→- WeTTY container: `sc-wetty-{user}-{sandbox}` on the `sandcastle-web` Docker network
   127→- SSH keypair: generated per-open in `/data/wetty/{full_name}/`, private key copied into WeTTY container via `docker exec` + base64 (bind mounts don't work because Rails runs inside a container with a named Docker volume for `/data`)
   128→- Traefik dynamic config: `terminal-{id}.yml` in `/data/traefik/dynamic/` with forwardAuth middleware
   129→- WeTTY uses `COMMAND` env var with full SSH command including tmux (`tmux new-session -A -s main`)
   130→- `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` env var controls the redirect target (needed when Rails is behind a different port than Traefik)
   131→
   132→**ForwardAuth flow (`TerminalController#auth`):**
   133→- Traefik sends each request to `http://sandcastle-web:80/terminal/auth` with `X-Forwarded-Uri`
   134→- Rails validates the session cookie and sandbox ownership (owner or admin)
   135→- Unauthenticated users get redirected to the login page with `return_to_after_authenticating` set to the terminal URL
   136→- Returns 200 to allow, redirect to login, or 401 for unauthorized
   137→
   138→**Important gotchas:**
   139→- Sandbox entrypoint must **append** SSH keys (not overwrite) because all sandboxes for the same user share `/home/{user}` via bind mount
   140→- `remove_pubkey` uses end-of-line regex anchor (`$`) to avoid substring collisions between sandbox names
   141→- Traefik config is written early in `TerminalManager#open` (before container creation) with a 1-second sleep to allow Traefik to detect the new route before the browser redirect arrives
   142→
   143→### Tailscale System
   144→
   145→Per-user Tailscale sidecars connect sandbox containers to a user's own tailnet. Each user gets their own sidecar joining **their** tailnet (not a shared one).
   146→
   147→**Two authentication flows in `TailscaleManager`:**
   148→- **Auth key flow** (`enable`): One-shot with `TS_AUTHKEY` env var, uses default `containerboot` entrypoint
   149→- **Interactive login flow** (`start_login` → `check_login`): Overrides entrypoint to `tailscaled` directly, runs `tailscale up --reset --timeout=10s` to get a browser login URL, polls `tailscale status --json` for `BackendState == "Running"`
   150→
   151→**User `tailscale_state` field:** `disabled` → `pending` (during interactive login) → `enabled`
   152→
   153→**Sidecar architecture:**
   154→- Bridge network: `sc-ts-net-{username}` with a random `/24` subnet drawn from `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE` (e.g. `10.89.42.0/24`)
   155→- Container: `sc-ts-{username}` running `tailscale/tailscale:latest`
   156→- State persisted at `/data/users/{name}/tailscale`
   157→- Advertises subnet routes so sandboxes on the bridge are reachable from the tailnet
   158→- `tailscale_auto_connect` user setting auto-joins new sandboxes to the bridge network
   159→
   160→**Important:** `containerboot` (default Tailscale image entrypoint) conflicts with manual `tailscale up` when no auth key is provided. The interactive flow must override the entrypoint to run `tailscaled` directly.
   161→
   162→### Networking Invariants — Do Not Change Without Asking
   163→
   164→The Dockyard Docker daemon (`sc_docker`) and the Rails app share a single private address space. **All of the following must stay consistent or Tailscale sidecars (and sandbox containers) will lose internet access.**
   165→
   166→```
   167→SANDCASTLE_PRIVATE_NET  (e.g. 10.89.0.0/16)
   168→└── DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE  = same /16  (passed to dockerd --default-address-pool)
   169→    ├── DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR  = first /24 (e.g. 10.89.0.1/24) — sc_docker0 bridge
   170→    ├── DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR   = same /24 (e.g. 10.89.0.0/24)  — static container IPs
   171→    ├── sandcastle-web network — allocated from the pool (e.g. 10.89.1.0/24)
   172→    └── sc-ts-net-{user}      — random /24 from the pool (e.g. 10.89.42.0/24)
   173→```
   174→
   175→**iptables rules** (set by the `sc_docker` systemd unit at startup, NOT by dockerd):
   176→- `POSTROUTING MASQUERADE` for the entire `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE` /16 → gives all containers internet via NAT
   177→- `FORWARD ACCEPT` for src/dst within the /16 → allows container-to-container and container-to-internet traffic
   178→
   179→**Critical constraints:**
   180→1. `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE` **must be passed** to the Rails `web` and `worker` containers as an env var — `subnet_for()` in `TailscaleManager` reads it to pick a subnet within the masqueraded range. If it is missing or mismatched, Tailscale sidecars get a subnet outside the MASQUERADE rule and have no internet.
   181→2. The Dockyard daemon runs with `--iptables=false` — it never touches iptables itself. All rules live in the systemd unit. **Do not add Docker iptables management** (`--iptables=true`) without updating the unit's ExecStartPre rules accordingly.
   182→3. `SANDCASTLE_PRIVATE_NET` / `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE` must not overlap with the host's existing routes. `gen-env` picks a non-conflicting /16 automatically — do not hard-code a range without checking.
   183→4. Changing `SANDCASTLE_PRIVATE_NET` on an existing install requires destroying all containers and networks first — existing containers have IPs baked in.
   184→
   185→### Background Jobs
   186→
   187→Solid Queue with recurring schedule in `config/recurring.yml`:
   188→- `ContainerSyncJob` — every 5 min, reconciles DB sandbox status with actual Docker container state and Tailscale sidecar health
   189→
   190→### Database
   191→
   192→PostgreSQL with 4 separate databases in production (`config/database.yml`):
   193→- `sandcastle_production` — primary (users, sandboxes, api_tokens, sessions)
   194→- `sandcastle_production_cache` — Solid Cache
   195→- `sandcastle_production_queue` — Solid Queue
   196→- `sandcastle_production_cable` — Solid Cable
   197→
   198→The primary database is created by PostgreSQL's `POSTGRES_DB` env var. The other 3 are created by a PostgreSQL init script (`docker/postgres/init-databases.sh`, mounted into `/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/`). The installer writes this script to `$REDACTED.sh`. When adding or removing Solid* databases, update both the init script and `database.yml`.
   199→
   200→**Database password preservation:**
   201→- Password stored in `$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/postgres/.secrets` (600 perms)
   202→- On fresh install: generates random password and saves to `.secrets`
   203→- On reinstall: reuses password from `.secrets` to match existing postgres data
   204→- Preserved during uninstall/reset (user data)
   205→- Prevents authentication failures when reinstalling with existing database
   206→
   207→Key constraints:
   208→- Sandbox names and SSH ports are unique only among non-destroyed sandboxes (partial unique indexes: `WHERE status != 'destroyed'`)
   209→- SSH port auto-assigned from 2201–2299 on create
   210→
   211→### Frontend
   212→
   213→ERB templates with Tailwind CSS (v4). Turbo Frames for async container stats. No heavy JS framework — Stimulus controllers only where needed. The Tailscale pending state uses vanilla JS polling (fetch every 3s) to detect auth completion.
   214→
   215→## Deployment
   216→
   217→- **Host**: `100.106.185.92` (Tailscale IP), SSH user `thies`; SSH alias `sandman`
   218→- **Sandcastle home on host**: `/sandcastle` (not `/data` — that's the data dir inside containers)
   219→- **Dockyard Docker CLI on host**: `/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker` (isolated daemon, not system Docker)
   220→  - To run docker commands on sandman: `ssh sandman "/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker ps"`
   221→- **Registry**: `ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle` (GitHub Container Registry)
   222→- **Deploy**: `docker-compose up -d` (see `docker-compose.yml`)
   223→- **Host bootstrap**: `bootstrap/sandcastle-bootstrap.sh` (Docker, Sysbox, Caddy, UFW)
   224→- **Env vars**: `SECRET_KEY_BASE`, `RAILS_MASTER_KEY`, `SANDCASTLE_HOST`, `SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR` (default `/data`)
   225→- **Proxy**: Traefik with `response_timeout: 60` (needed for Tailscale login flow which blocks ~13s)
   226→- The app container needs `group-add: 988` for Docker socket access
   227→
   228→## Conventions
   229→
   230→- When features are changed or added, update the guide page at `app/views/pages/guide.html.erb` to reflect the new CLI commands and usage.
   231→- **Never edit `installer.sh` directly** — it is generated from `installer/installer.sh.in`. Make all installer changes in `installer/installer.sh.in` and regenerate `installer.sh` from it.
   232→
   233→## GitHub Actions Workflow
   234→
   235→When triggered via `@claude` comments in GitHub issues or PRs:
   236→
   237→- **Always complete the full workflow** without asking for approval to commit/push/create PRs
   238→- The workflow grants explicit permissions (`--allowedTools`) for git operations
   239→- After implementing changes:
   240→  1. Stage files with `git add`
   241→  2. Commit with descriptive message including `Co-authored-by` trailer
   242→  3. Push to the branch immediately
   243→  4. Provide a link to create a PR (or create it if you have the capability)
   244→- **Do not stop and ask for permission** — the `--allowedTools` configuration is the permission
   245→- Only ask for clarification if requirements are unclear, not for commit/push approval
   246→
```

> AGENT

Traefik is routing based on `Host(`dev.sand`)`, not `localhost`. You need to either:

**Option A** — Use `dev.sand` (add to `/etc/hosts`):
```
127.0.0.1 dev.sand
```
Then access `http://dev.sand:8080` or `https://dev.sand:8443`.

**Option B** — Hit the Rails container directly, bypassing Traefik:
```bash
curl http://localhost:8080 -H "Host: dev.sand"
```

Option A is the easiest — want me to check what `DEV_HOST` is set to and where it comes from?

```
     1→import { Controller } from "@hotwired/stimulus"
     2→
     3→export default class extends Controller {
     4→  static values = { metricsUrl: String, statsUrl: String }
     5→  static targets = ["cpuChart", "memChart", "cpuValue", "memValue"]
     6→
     7→  connect() {
     8→    this.cpuData = []
     9→    this.memData = []
    10→    this.windowMs = 30 * 60 * 1000
    11→
    12→    this.loadHistory()
    13→    this.pollInterval = setInterval(() => this.pollStats(), 5000)
    14→  }
    15→
    16→  disconnect() {
    17→    if (this.pollInterval) clearInterval(this.pollInterval)
    18→  }
    19→
    20→  async loadHistory() {
    21→    try {
    22→      const resp = await fetch(this.metricsUrlValue)
    23→      if (!resp.ok) return
    24→      const points = await resp.json()
    25→      points.forEach(p => {
    26→        this.cpuData.push({ t: p.t * 1000, v: p.cpu })
    27→        this.memData.push({ t: p.t * 1000, v: p.mem })
    28→      })
    29→      this.render()
    30→    } catch (e) {
    31→      // ignore
    32→    }
    33→  }
    34→
    35→  async pollStats() {
    36→    try {
    37→      const resp = await fetch(this.statsUrlValue)
    38→      if (!resp.ok) return
    39→      const data = await resp.json()
    40→      if (data.cpu == null) return
    41→      const now = Date.now()
    42→      this.cpuData.push({ t: now, v: data.cpu })
    43→      this.memData.push({ t: now, v: data.mem })
    44→      this.trim()
    45→      this.render()
    46→    } catch (e) {
    47→      // ignore
    48→    }
    49→  }
    50→
    51→  trim() {
    52→    const cutoff = Date.now() - this.windowMs
    53→    this.cpuData = this.cpuData.filter(p => p.t > cutoff)
    54→    this.memData = this.memData.filter(p => p.t > cutoff)
    55→  }
    56→
    57→  render() {
    58→    this.renderChart(this.cpuChartTarget, this.cpuData, 0, 100)
    59→    this.renderChart(this.memChartTarget, this.memData, 0, null)
    60→
    61→    const lastCpu = this.cpuData[this.cpuData.length - 1]
    62→    const lastMem = this.memData[this.memData.length - 1]
    63→    if (lastCpu) this.cpuValueTarget.textContent = `${lastCpu.v.toFixed(1)}%`
    64→    if (lastMem) this.memValueTarget.textContent = `${Math.round(lastMem.v)} MB`
    65→  }
    66→
    67→  renderChart(target, data, fixedMin, fixedMax) {
    68→    if (data.length < 2) {
    69→      target.innerHTML = ""
    70→      return
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    const w = 200, h = 36, pad = 1
    74→    const min = fixedMin != null ? fixedMin : Math.min(...data.map(p => p.v))
    75→    let max = fixedMax != null ? fixedMax : Math.max(...data.map(p => p.v))
    76→    if (max === min) max = min + 1
    77→
    78→    const tMin = data[0].t, tMax = data[data.length - 1].t
    79→    const tRange = tMax - tMin || 1
    80→
    81→    const points = data.map(p => {
    82→      const x = pad + ((p.t - tMin) / tRange) * (w - 2 * pad)
    83→      const y = h - pad - ((p.v - min) / (max - min)) * (h - 2 * pad)
    84→      return `${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`
    85→    }).join(" ")
    86→
    87→    const lastPoint = data[data.length - 1]
    88→    const lastX = pad + ((lastPoint.t - tMin) / tRange) * (w - 2 * pad)
    89→    const lastY = h - pad - ((lastPoint.v - min) / (max - min)) * (h - 2 * pad)
    90→
    91→    const fillPoints = `${pad},${h} ${points} ${lastX.toFixed(1)},${h}`
    92→
    93→    const isBlue = fixedMax === 100
    94→    const strokeColor = isBlue ? "#3b82f6" : "#8b5cf6"
    95→    const fillColor = isBlue ? "rgba(59,130,246,0.1)" : "rgba(139,92,246,0.1)"
    96→
    97→    target.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" class="w-full h-9" preserveAspectRatio="none">
    98→      <polygon points="${fillPoints}" fill="${fillColor}" />
    99→      <polyline points="${points}" fill="none" stroke="${strokeColor}" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round" />
   100→      <circle cx="${lastX.toFixed(1)}" cy="${lastY.toFixed(1)}" r="2" fill="${strokeColor}" />
   101→    </svg>`
   102→  }
   103→}
   104→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/javascript/controllers/sparkline_controller.js",
  "content": "import { Controller } from \"@hotwired/stimulus\"\n\nexport default class extends Controller {\n  static values = { metricsUrl: String, statsUrl: String, compact: { type: Boolean, default: false } }\n  static targets = [\"cpuChart\", \"memChart\", \"cpuValue\", \"memValue\"]\n\n  connect() {\n    this.cpuData = []\n    this.memData = []\n    this.windowMs = 30 * 60 * 1000\n\n    this.loadHistory()\n    this.pollInterval = setInterval(() => this.pollStats(), 5000)\n  }\n\n  disconnect() {\n    if (this.pollInterval) clearInterval(this.pollInterval)\n  }\n\n  async loadHistory() {\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch(this.metricsUrlValue)\n      if (!resp.ok) return\n      const points = await resp.json()\n      points.forEach(p => {\n        this.cpuData.push({ t: p.t * 1000, v: p.cpu })\n        this.memData.push({ t: p.t * 1000, v: p.mem })\n      })\n      this.render()\n    } catch (e) {\n      // ignore\n    }\n  }\n\n  async pollStats() {\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch(this.statsUrlValue)\n      if (!resp.ok) return\n      const data = await resp.json()\n      if (data.cpu == null) return\n      const now = Date.now()\n      this.cpuData.push({ t: now, v: data.cpu })\n      this.memData.push({ t: now, v: data.mem })\n      this.trim()\n      this.render()\n    } catch (e) {\n      // ignore\n    }\n  }\n\n  trim() {\n    const cutoff = Date.now() - this.windowMs\n    this.cpuData = this.cpuData.filter(p => p.t > cutoff)\n    this.memData = this.memData.filter(p => p.t > cutoff)\n  }\n\n  render() {\n    const compact = this.compactValue\n    if (this.hasCpuChartTarget) this.renderChart(this.cpuChartTarget, this.cpuData, 0, 100, compact)\n    if (this.hasMemChartTarget) this.renderChart(this.memChartTarget, this.memData, 0, null, compact)\n\n    const lastCpu = this.cpuData[this.cpuData.length - 1]\n    const lastMem = this.memData[this.memData.length - 1]\n    if (lastCpu && this.hasCpuValueTarget) this.cpuValueTarget.textContent = `${lastCpu.v.toFixed(1)}%`\n    if (lastMem && this.hasMemValueTarget) this.memValueTarget.textContent = `${Math.round(lastMem.v)} MB`\n  }\n\n  renderChart(target, data, fixedMin, fixedMax, compact) {\n    if (data.length === 0) {\n      target.innerHTML = \"\"\n      return\n    }\n\n    const w = compact ? 80 : 200\n    const h = compact ? 20 : 36\n    const pad = 1\n    const min = fixedMin != null ? fixedMin : Math.min(...data.map(p => p.v))\n    let max = fixedMax != null ? fixedMax : Math.max(...data.map(p => p.v))\n    if (max === min) max = min + 1\n\n    const isBlue = fixedMax === 100\n    const strokeColor = isBlue ? \"#3b82f6\" : \"#8b5cf6\"\n    const fillColor = isBlue ? \"rgba(59,130,246,0.1)\" : \"rgba(139,92,246,0.1)\"\n    const heightClass = compact ? \"h-5\" : \"h-9\"\n\n    // Single point: just show a dot\n    if (data.length === 1) {\n      const y = h - pad - ((data[0].v - min) / (max - min)) * (h - 2 * pad)\n      target.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox=\"0 0 ${w} ${h}\" class=\"w-full ${heightClass}\" preserveAspectRatio=\"none\">\n        <circle cx=\"${w / 2}\" cy=\"${y.toFixed(1)}\" r=\"2\" fill=\"${strokeColor}\" />\n      </svg>`\n      return\n    }\n\n    const tMin = data[0].t, tMax = data[data.length - 1].t\n    const tRange = tMax - tMin || 1\n\n    const points = data.map(p => {\n      const x = pad + ((p.t - tMin) / tRange) * (w - 2 * pad)\n      const y = h - pad - ((p.v - min) / (max - min)) * (h - 2 * pad)\n      return `${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`\n    }).join(\" \")\n\n    const lastPoint = data[data.length - 1]\n    const lastX = pad + ((lastPoint.t - tMin) / tRange) * (w - 2 * pad)\n    const lastY = h - pad - ((lastPoint.v - min) / (max - min)) * (h - 2 * pad)\n\n    const fillPoints = `${pad},${h} ${points} ${lastX.toFixed(1)},${h}`\n    const sw = compact ? 1 : 1.5\n    const r = compact ? 1.5 : 2\n\n    target.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox=\"0 0 ${w} ${h}\" class=\"w-full ${heightClass}\" preserveAspectRatio=\"none\">\n      <polygon points=\"${fillPoints}\" fill=\"${fillColor}\" />\n      <polyline points=\"${points}\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"${strokeColor}\" stroke-width=\"${sw}\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" />\n      <circle cx=\"${lastX.toFixed(1)}\" cy=\"${lastY.toFixed(1)}\" r=\"${r}\" fill=\"${strokeColor}\" />\n    </svg>`\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "old_string": "  def metrics\n    points = @sandbox.container_metrics.recent\n    render json: points.map { |m| { t: m.recorded_at.to_i, cpu: m.cpu_percent, mem: m.memory_mb.round(0) } }\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def metrics\n    points = @sandbox.container_metrics.recent\n      .map { |m| { t: m.recorded_at.to_i, cpu: m.cpu_percent, mem: m.memory_mb.round(0) } }\n\n    # Append current live stats so graphs render instantly on page load\n    if @sandbox.status == \"running\" && @sandbox.container_id.present?\n      live = Rails.cache.fetch(\"sandbox_stats:#{@sandbox.id}\", expires_in: 4.seconds) do\n        raw = Docker::Container.get(@sandbox.container_id).stats(stream: false)\n        raw.present? ? { cpu: StatsCalculator.cpu_percent(raw), mem: StatsCalculator.memory_mb(raw).round(0) } : nil\n      rescue Docker::Error::DockerError\n        nil\n      end\n      points << { t: Time.current.to_i, **live } if live\n    end\n\n    render json: points\n  end"
}
```

> AGENT

The stats endpoint calls `Docker::Container.stats(stream: false)` which is a blocking Docker API call. I'll cache the full stats result so both the JSON response and the metrics endpoint share the same cache entry.

> TOOL

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/dashboard_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "    if sandbox.status == \"running\" && sandbox.container_id.present?\n      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)\n      raw = container.stats(stream: false)\n\n      # Handle case where container is shutting down and returns nil/incomplete stats\n      if raw.present?\n        networks = raw[\"networks\"] || {}\n        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n[\"rx_bytes\"] || 0 }\n        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n[\"tx_bytes\"] || 0 }\n\n        blkio = raw.dig(\"blkio_stats\", \"io_service_bytes_recursive\") || []\n        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e[\"op\"]&.downcase == \"read\" }.sum { |e| e[\"value\"] || 0 }\n        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e[\"op\"]&.downcase == \"write\" }.sum { |e| e[\"value\"] || 0 }\n\n        @stats = {\n          cpu_percent: StatsCalculator.cpu_percent(raw),\n          memory_mb: StatsCalculator.memory_mb(raw),\n          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig(\"memory_stats\", \"limit\") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,\n          net_rx: net_rx,\n          net_tx: net_tx,\n          disk_read: disk_read,\n          disk_write: disk_write,\n          pids: raw.dig(\"pids_stats\", \"current\") || 0\n        }\n      end\n    end",
  "new_string": "    if sandbox.status == \"running\" && sandbox.container_id.present?\n      @stats = Rails.cache.fetch(\"sandbox_stats_full:#{sandbox.id}\", expires_in: 4.seconds) do\n        container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)\n        raw = container.stats(stream: false)\n\n        next nil if raw.blank?\n\n        networks = raw[\"networks\"] || {}\n        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n[\"rx_bytes\"] || 0 }\n        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n[\"tx_bytes\"] || 0 }\n\n        blkio = raw.dig(\"blkio_stats\", \"io_service_bytes_recursive\") || []\n        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e[\"op\"]&.downcase == \"read\" }.sum { |e| e[\"value\"] || 0 }\n        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e[\"op\"]&.downcase == \"write\" }.sum { |e| e[\"value\"] || 0 }\n\n        {\n          cpu_percent: StatsCalculator.cpu_percent(raw),\n          memory_mb: StatsCalculator.memory_mb(raw),\n          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig(\"memory_stats\", \"limit\") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,\n          net_rx: net_rx,\n          net_tx: net_tx,\n          disk_read: disk_read,\n          disk_write: disk_write,\n          pids: raw.dig(\"pids_stats\", \"current\") || 0\n        }\n      end\n    end"
```
